import MeetingCallout from '~/components/vqeg/MeetingCallout.astro';
import MeetingHero from '~/components/vqeg/MeetingHero.astro';

<MeetingHero>

Face-to-face meetings are held approximately twice per year. Meetings are open to the public and hosted by VQEG members. During VQEG meetings, expect:

- Progress report on VQEG projects
- Calls for participation
- Presentations on state-of-the-art research
- Debates on best practices
- Discussions among video quality experts

</MeetingHero>

<MeetingCallout>
  <div slot="venues">

## Future Meeting Venues

Meetings shift from country to country

- Winter VQEG meeting, 14-18 December 2026, <a href="/meetings/virtual" target="" data-router-slot="disabled" title="VQEG Meeting Virtual">online meeting details</a>
- Spring VQEG meeting, 24-27 May 2027, hosted by AT&T in New York, USA, <a href="/meetings/in-person" target="" data-router-slot="disabled" title="VQEG Meeting Information">in-person meeting details</a>

</div>

VQEG seeks presentations and discussions on research related to video quality, but not necessarily within the scope of any active VQEG work. All participants are invited to share their specialized needs or insights around video quality.

Send questions about the upcoming meeting to [meeting@vqeg.org](mailto:meeting@vqeg.org). Your email will be forwarded to the meeting host and the VQEG co-chairs.

Information for meeting hosts is available <a href="https://docs.google.com/document/d/1xVkDPHsCtrPLW-DSU0G043M8rLOjd3_TX31Pbc6YwEI/edit?usp=sharing" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-anchor="?usp=sharing">here</a>.

</MeetingCallout>
